P	[ACPOS-20260921-EXACT-BINDING-CLOSURE] Construction Authority Notice
本文件已加入 2026-09-21 Exact Binding Closure。施工時 MUST 讀取文末 Closure Annex；文中較早的 REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING 對已列入 Resolution Ledger 的項目視為已被本次 closure 精確綁定。外部 secret/provider/platform 的實際值只可由已註冊 Runtime Profile / Environment Binding 解析，缺值 = BLOCK，不得猜。
P	ACPOS-AI-09
P	AI System Engineer / Self-Inspection / Evolution System
P	AI 系統工程師／自我檢查／演化系統
P	Mother Basic Logic Design + Normative Logic Contract
P	圖 1｜AI System Engineer 受治理生命週期；自我檢查可自動化，但 Authority 採用與 effectful execution 不得自我批准。
P	Design Status: DESIGN_CONTENT_COMPLETE · FORMAL_APPROVAL_NOT_SELF_GRANTED · RUNTIME_NOT_PROVEN_BY_DOCUMENT
P	Current design basis: rebuild-v2.1.1 · HEAD 9b25fe3ab4e304103d9b6980123d25bb2d2fadb3 · Governance GOV-REV-20260920-CROSS-STAGE-MATERIALIZATION-CONSUMER-READINESS-HARDENING (display v2.2.16)
P	1. Design Identity / 設計定位
P	核心定位
P	• 本文件是 ACPOS 全站 AI System Engineer、Self-Inspection、Self-Repair Proposal 與 Governed Evolution 的唯一系統邏輯 Owner。
P	• 它負責重新解析 Current Truth、追蹤完整功能鏈、建立 Finding、分析 Root Cause、解析 Canonical Owner、計算 Change Impact、產生 Change Candidate、Simulation/Validation、準備 Authorized Execution、Deployment Verification、Rollback、Resume 與 Incident Learning。
P	• 它不是 Current Authority 本身；AI 的 Finding、Root Cause、Patch Proposal、Simulation Result 或 Test PASS 都只是 Evidence/Candidate，不能自動升格為 Authority。
P	• AI MAY inspect / classify / propose / simulate / verify；AI MUST_NOT self-approve、self-promote Current Governance、self-bypass Gate、self-claim Production PASS。

P	27. Concurrency / Version / Idempotency

P	Same key different payload

P	deployment/mutation gate MUST serialize，避免兩個 Current。

P	30. Security / Classification / Secrets
P	• System inspection MUST honor actor/project/object classification and permission scope；診斷便利性不得繞過資料隔離。
P	• AI-09 MUST NOT emit raw secrets/tokens/credentials into prompt, log, finding, evidence, error, export or Word。
P	• Secret handling MUST use reference/secret manager identity；lack of exact secret binding = REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING。
P	• Production write, deployment, permission/RLS/security change MUST require stronger authorization than read-only inspection。
P	• Security Finding SHOULD trigger containment before ordinary remediation planning when active risk exists。
P	• Inspection evidence containing sensitive data MUST carry classification/retention/redaction requirements。
P	• Cross-project fallback or cross-tenant evidence reuse without authority MUST be BLOCK。
P	• AI MUST NOT weaken gate/permission/classification to make validation pass。

P	37. Construction Handoff / Closure Contract
P	Implementation handoff MUST preserve the following non-negotiable semantics:
P	• One Current Truth Bootstrap path；不得由每個 inspector 自己解讀 current version。
P	• One Finding / Problem Register truth；scanner/validator/remediator 不得各自維護不同 denominator。
P	• One Canonical Owner resolution model；修正 owner，不建立平行副本。
P	• Functional Chain 必須可從 Page/Trigger 追到 Runtime/Data/Provider，再回 Audit/Response/UI Feedback。
P	• Change Candidate 與 Current Authority 必須物理/語義分離；candidate 不可自動成 Current。
P	• AI-09 不得擁有自己的自批權；Authorization 必須由外部授權 identity 完成。
P	• Simulation/Test/Deploy/Production 四層證據不得互相替代。
P	• Rollback/Resume/Checkpoint 必須與 effectful execution 一起施工，不是事後補文件。
P	• Incident Learning 與 EvolutionCandidate 只能透過 Governed Adoption 改善未來規則。
P	• 所有缺失 exact API/DB/permission/route/runtime identity 必須維持 REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING，直到 Current Authority 實際存在。
ROW	Closure item	Required terminal condition
P	Closure item
P	Required terminal condition
ROW	Design Content	本文件所有 owner boundary / invariant / state / event / acceptance 已定義
P	Design Content
P	本文件所有 owner boundary / invariant / state / event / acceptance 已定義

P	High 0 / Medium 0 / Low 0

P	Design Closure Boundary 這三份補齊文件只完成設計層閉環；GitHub 程式碼、Runtime、E2E 與 Production 狀態仍必須由後續 fresh execution evidence 個別證明。
P	G. Construction Handoff Rule
P	施工 AI MUST resolve each UI control through this exact order: Page UID → Section/Region → Control UID → Trigger/Operation or Action UID → Gate → Permission → Input/Payload → Method/Path or shared Runtime Operation → Persistence/Provider Owner → Audit/Event → Success/Failure/Recovery → Next Page/Handoff. UI_ONLY/READ_ONLY controls MUST NOT create a write API. System-only operations MUST NOT cause a new visible button unless this closure/source explicitly materializes that control.
P	When a provider/platform/environment value is runtime-specific, the exact binding is the registered Profile/Policy/Environment key named in this annex. Missing required value is a deterministic BLOCK; it is not permission to guess.
P	Unified Page / Control / Action / API / DB / Runtime Construction Matrix
P	[ACPOS-20260921-UNIFIED-CONTROL-RUNTIME-MATRIX] This matrix is the direct construction lookup. Existing source identifiers are preserved. New Action/Gate/Control identities are created only when exhaustive uploaded-source scan found the semantic control/operation but no exact UID/path. Existing exact Method/Path always wins; otherwise effectful operations use POST /v1/commands/{actionUid} through the Shared Typed Command Runtime. UI/read-only controls explicitly bind to N/A or projection runtime and MUST NOT create writes.
